## Summary

Individually assessed all four actual native source targets and five occurrences against current owner bytes and captured sources. Source-1 barley and source-2 sugar-beet arabinan support bounded same-material source-to-stable-MIM identity. Source-3 estradiol and source-4 MEM remain WITHHOLD for their existing substantive obligations. Two open major hold observations remain; no external mapping, role, source correction, production publication or KG approval is granted.

## Scope And Provenance

Full scientific primary review of this explicit four-source canary, with every declared occurrence and exact proposed current owner; complete campaign inputs are bound as provenance, not a claim to have scientifically re-reviewed the other sources.

Selection: Exact ordered native targets from actual request b6c76f85 and completed source inspection dc7c987c. Includes three complete CultureMech material-reference scopes and the complete two-occurrence MediaDive1923 scope.
Coverage: full; 4 reviewed / 4 in the declared population.
Source: working_tree at Git base 6677f1e1679bec82f5af5e125454d4b7852780e9.

## Scientific And Domain Assessments

### Entire barley source material and current permanent owner

identity: supported. Targets: source-1.

Same material within the complete declared source scope: the sole CultureMech:000043 occurrence denotes the barley grain input before the separate autoclaving step, matching current MIM:0002720 Barley grains. The positive decision is grounded in the CCAP recipe and complete occurrence/current owner, not merely the singular/plural alias or permanent ID.

Retain UNMAPPED_0096 and UNMAPPED externally; source identity can be MIM-only. Preserve one grain per 25 mL, VARIABLE representation and pre-autoclave addition. No mass, cultivar, sterility, procurement grade, taxonomic/food identity or identity to MIM:0002721 autoclaved grains is inferred. Full-recipe inherited annotations remain outside this material claim.

### Entire sugar-beet arabinan source and botanical-qualified permanent owner

identity: supported. Targets: source-2.

Same material at the reviewed botanical-qualified ingredient scope: the sole complete CultureMech:008900 occurrence explicitly names arabinan (sugar beet), and current MIM:0000366 retains sugar-beet arabinan. Captured manufacturer material content corroborates that scope. No grade, lot, linear/debranched fraction or other-source equivalence is inferred.

CAS 11078-27-6 does not prove P-ARAB/P-LARB equivalence; the archived capture explicitly describes distinct linear material. Preserve the current CHEBI:22590 broader link as a separate mapping, not a source identity or subclass edge. The normalized recipe identifies an alternative carbon source rather than simultaneous mixture ingredients; no concentration/role/complete-recipe or complete external-row release approval is conferred.

### 17beta-estradiol material evidence with the original quantity/export hold

identity: concern. Targets: source-3.

WITHHOLD the source identity candidate while retaining current proposed owner MIM:0002957. NBRC and ChEBI support the beta-isomer chemistry, but the complete frozen occurrence still says 1 g/L and Growth factor whereas NBRC lists 1 mg with 1 L water. The original combined quantity/assay-role/export obligation remains literal; chemically plausible identity cannot clear it.

The exact hold is NBRC1181 prints1mg whereas frozen normalization says1g/L; no quantity, assay-role or export approval. No concentration correction, role approval, owner change or inherited publication support is made. The existing CHEBI:16469 mapping remains a separately reviewed external claim and cannot rescue this source hold.

### Whole MediaDive 1923 scope across both captured MEM formulations

identity: concern. Targets: source-4.

WITHHOLD the global MediaDive 1923 source identity. Current generic MIM:0002840 is a historically linked proposed owner, not an established whole-source exact identity. One raw occurrence specifies BioWest P0440 and the other only BioWest; the owner explicitly awaits formulation-specific curation. Captured manufacturer evidence establishes P0440 form but does not establish that all source use and the generic owner are interchangeable.

This is not a claim of proven difference merely because the second catalogue number is absent. The specific P0440 evidence is narrower than the unresolved whole source/current generic owner. Preserve 0.89 g/8.9 g_l in solution 6374 and 5.2 g/130 g_l in solution 4890 plus volume/preparation context. The 40 mL stored volume and step to 584 mL are not silently reconciled. Manufacturer 10.131 g/L reconstitution is not the microbial recipe dose. No bicarbonate/glutamine/form equivalence, external identifier, role, component decomposition or source split is approved.

### Declared source completeness and namespace meaning

scope: supported. Targets: source-1, source-2, source-3, source-4.

The actual four-row canary includes every five matching occurrence from the complete copied source tables. Three MIMCMREFv1 IDs reconstruct from each exact original complete case plus the immutable declaration; they remain versioned MIM-generated material-reference scopes, not CultureMech-issued IDs or permanent ingredient IDs. The original 1879-case/1022-origin context remains bound and is not replaced by this canary.

### Permanent identity, inexact mappings and consumer namespace

graph: supported. Targets: source-1, source-2, source-3, source-4.

Current permanent IDs remain distinct from source reference IDs and legacy mapping identifiers. A supported barley identity needs no external ontology parent. Arabinan external broadMatch remains separately typed; beta-estradiol chemistry does not clear source holds; MEM remains without an invented external match. MIM.ingredient spelling preserves the same declared permanent URI as MIM and original CURIE provenance. Neither exact source identity nor any broader/close/narrow mapping authorizes subclass_of or owner collapse.

## Findings

### ESTRADIOL-SOURCE-QUANTITY-HOLD: Retained estradiol source dose and associated export obligation remain unresolved

major / open / confirmed; issue key: MIM-ESTRADIOL-1181-SOURCE-QUANTITY-HOLD.

This is a current observation of the existing substantive hold, not a new owner-identity defect or a claim that the canary introduced it. NBRC 1181 gives 1 mg with 1 L water; the immutable source occurrence says 1 g/L and includes an unapproved Growth factor note. Keep the proposed source assertion WITHHOLD, preserve both exact payloads, and obtain a separately authorized maintained-source correction and scientific review before lifting the obligation.

### MEM-1923-WHOLE-SOURCE-IDENTITY-HOLD: Retained whole-source MEM formulation identity requires scope reconciliation

major / open / confirmed; issue key: MIM-MEM-1923-WHOLE-SOURCE-IDENTITY-HOLD.

This retains the already-recorded unresolved whole-source obligation. Product evidence supports P0440 powder with Earle salts/L-glutamine and no bicarbonate, but current generic owner MIM:0002840 and both source occurrences have not been established as one exact formulation scope. The second BioWest-only occurrence does not by absence alone prove a different product. Do not split the source, select only the P0440 occurrence, infer a formulation, or clear the hold from a minted ID or historical imported source ID.

## Recommended Actions And Acceptance Checks

### retain-and-resolve-estradiol-hold

Retain the exact existing WITHHOLD obligation. If source correction is separately authorized, reconcile the normalized quantity and unsupported role annotation against NBRC while preserving the old frozen case; make a newly versioned complete source reference and independently review its new payload.

- Native review continues to withhold the old unchanged source payload while its hold remains.
- Any new source payload preserves full historical provenance and receives independent whole-source scientific review; no owner identity or source hash is silently reused.

### retain-and-resolve-mem-hold

Keep both captured MEM occurrences and the generic owner in withheld accounting until primary evidence resolves complete supplied formulation scope. Reconcile historical generic owner versus product-specific use without guessing, owner merging, or per-occurrence source invention.

- Primary evidence supports or refutes one material scope across every occurrence and the exact current owner.
- All original quantities/preparation context and historical holds remain visible; any proposed correction uses guarded native curation and new independent review.

## Limits And Additional Notes

- This primary report does not substitute for the distinct skeptical reviewer, actual native source export validation, complete ordinary external SSSOM partition, publication technical acceptance or production/consumer authorization.
- All source-3/source-4 literal holds remain in native payloads. The new findings are observations of those retained obligations, not evidence that the current canary introduced them or authorization to close an old issue.
- Positive source-1/source-2 decisions do not authorize count-to-mass conversion, source-dose/role assertions, procurement-grade equivalence, taxonomic/food matching, generic-parent identity, owner merging or any other recipe annotations.
- The Arabinan manufacturer evidence is an archived extracted primary-content capture, not a new raw PDF download. It supports botanical material identity only; shared CAS never proves linear P-LARB and P-ARAB equivalence.
- Captured primary/authority text was inspected without fresh paid provider research. No live OAK term check, whole-corpus QC or future output validator is claimed here. Those global checks remain coordinator-owned and passed checks were not repeated.
- Complete accounting is established for the exact supplied tables. This does not certify completeness of any upstream provider database or turn a MIM-generated material reference into an upstream identifier.
- Current owner/source bytes withdraw old support; no prior W01/W09 report, old filename MIM alias or stable allocation is treated as current source identity approval.
- Native inspection ran after actual source inspection succeeded and after adoption; no provisional-stage vote was promoted by replacing hashes.
- Private readback retained setup error cadab3 (incorrect guessed stable_ids.py directory) and exploratory parse error 33ea92 (NBRC HTML stored with .json suffix); neither was a target-read denial or failed native scientific check. Correct file discovery and direct HTML reading succeeded before these judgments.
- No shared record-review check is run by this primary author; the coordinator runs it once after the actual primary and skeptical pairs exist.
- First unpublished serialization validation failed in tool07c747 because PyYAML emitted anchors for shared Python list objects. The original draft is retained. This successor expands those aliases without changing scientific conclusions, targets or inputs; native schema/helper were unchanged.
- Second unpublished validation576dc4 caught a review_id/updated-finish timestamp mismatch in the serialization successor. The ID is now derived from the actual stated finish timestamp, without changing conclusions, inputs or native checks; both earlier drafts remain.

scope:
  description: Full scientific primary review of this explicit four-source canary,
    with every declared occurrence and exact proposed current owner; complete campaign
    inputs are bound as provenance, not a claim to have scientifically re-reviewed
    the other sources.
  selection: Exact ordered native targets from actual request b6c76f85 and completed
    source inspection dc7c987c. Includes three complete CultureMech material-reference
    scopes and the complete two-occurrence MediaDive1923 scope.
  coverage: full
  population_size: 4
  reviewed_target_ids:
  - source-1
  - source-2
  - source-3
  - source-4
  exclusions:
  - target: Remaining original campaign cases and origins
    reason: Original 1879 cases/1022 origins and full source tables remain bound;
      this explicit canary assigns no new scientific disposition to unselected cases.
  - target: Canonical/external SSSOM rows, W01/W09 historical report claims and generated
      publication payloads
    reason: These require their separate exact-payload review; this source identity
      review neither restamps historical reviews nor supplies missing publication
      approval.
  - target: Roles, recipe quantity changes, formulation components, subclass assertions
      and KG rebuild
    reason: Outside the narrow source identity request; substantive holds and full
      historical claim boundaries remain.

    definition: The entire original source case and all one of its captured occurrences
      are assessed, without an occurrence split.
    evidence_ids:
    - source-nodes
    - source-occurrences
    - full-occurrences
  - name: source_identity_disposition
    value: same_material
    definition: Positive source-to-current-MIM material identity only; no ontology,
      role, quantity conversion or publication approval.
    evidence_ids:
    - owner-1
    - barley-ccap
    - barley-recipe
  details: Retain UNMAPPED_0096 and UNMAPPED externally; source identity can be MIM-only.
    Preserve one grain per 25 mL, VARIABLE representation and pre-autoclave addition.
    No mass, cultivar, sterility, procurement grade, taxonomic/food identity or identity
    to MIM:0002721 autoclaved grains is inferred. Full-recipe inherited annotations
    remain outside this material claim.
- assessment_id: identity-2
  area: identity
  topic: Entire sugar-beet arabinan source and botanical-qualified permanent owner
  outcome: supported
  target_ids:
  - source-2
  summary: 'Same material at the reviewed botanical-qualified ingredient scope: the
    sole complete CultureMech:008900 occurrence explicitly names arabinan (sugar beet),
    and current MIM:0000366 retains sugar-beet arabinan. Captured manufacturer material
    content corroborates that scope. No grade, lot, linear/debranched fraction or
    other-source equivalence is inferred.'
  evidence_ids:
  - native-payloads
  - source-occurrences
  - owner-2
  - arabinan-recipe
  - arabinan-manufacturer
  - arabinan-chebi
  - arabinan-sibling
  - prior-scope
  - semantics
  dimensions:
  - name: source_identity_scope
    value: all_source_occurrences
    definition: All one occurrence and the original complete source case are assessed,
      with source qualifiers retained.
    evidence_ids:
    - source-nodes
    - source-occurrences
    - full-occurrences
  - name: source_identity_disposition
    value: same_material
    definition: Positive source-to-current-MIM material identity with botanical scope
      retained; external mapping support is a separate decision.
    evidence_ids:
    - owner-2
    - arabinan-recipe
    - arabinan-manufacturer
  details: CAS 11078-27-6 does not prove P-ARAB/P-LARB equivalence; the archived capture
    explicitly describes distinct linear material. Preserve the current CHEBI:22590
    broader link as a separate mapping, not a source identity or subclass edge. The
    normalized recipe identifies an alternative carbon source rather than simultaneous
    mixture ingredients; no concentration/role/complete-recipe or complete external-row
    release approval is conferred.
- assessment_id: identity-3
  area: identity
  topic: 17beta-estradiol material evidence with the original quantity/export hold
  outcome: concern
  target_ids:
  - source-3
  summary: WITHHOLD the source identity candidate while retaining current proposed
    owner MIM:0002957. NBRC and ChEBI support the beta-isomer chemistry, but the complete
    frozen occurrence still says 1 g/L and Growth factor whereas NBRC lists 1 mg with
    1 L water. The original combined quantity/assay-role/export obligation remains
    literal; chemically plausible identity cannot clear it.
  evidence_ids:
  - native-payloads
  - owner-3
  - estradiol-nbrc
  - estradiol-chebi
  - estradiol-recipe
  - prior-scope
  - semantics
  dimensions:
  - name: source_identity_scope
    value: all_source_occurrences
    definition: The entire source case and its sole occurrence, including contradictory
      dose and role note, are included.
    evidence_ids:
    - source-nodes
    - source-occurrences
    - estradiol-recipe
  - name: source_identity_disposition
    value: withhold
    definition: Retain substantive unresolved scientific obligation and no supported
      source row; do not erase it because current chemistry is specific.
    evidence_ids:
    - native-payloads
    - prior-scope
    - estradiol-nbrc
    - estradiol-recipe
  details: The exact hold is NBRC1181 prints1mg whereas frozen normalization says1g/L;
    no quantity, assay-role or export approval. No concentration correction, role
    approval, owner change or inherited publication support is made. The existing
    CHEBI:16469 mapping remains a separately reviewed external claim and cannot rescue
    this source hold.
- assessment_id: identity-4
  area: identity
  topic: Whole MediaDive 1923 scope across both captured MEM formulations
  outcome: concern
  target_ids:
  - source-4
  summary: WITHHOLD the global MediaDive 1923 source identity. Current generic MIM:0002840
    is a historically linked proposed owner, not an established whole-source exact
    identity. One raw occurrence specifies BioWest P0440 and the other only BioWest;
    the owner explicitly awaits formulation-specific curation. Captured manufacturer
    evidence establishes P0440 form but does not establish that all source use and
    the generic owner are interchangeable.
  evidence_ids:
  - native-payloads
  - source-occurrences
  - owner-4
  - mem-specification
  - mem-product
  - mem-mediadive
  - prior-scope
  - semantics
  dimensions:
  - name: source_identity_scope
    value: all_source_occurrences
    definition: Both direct raw recipe occurrences and their complete enclosing solution
      contexts are assessed together.
    evidence_ids:
    - source-occurrences
    - full-occurrences
    - native-payloads
  - name: source_identity_disposition
    value: withhold
    definition: Whole-source identity remains unresolved; no positive P0440 subset
      or invented occurrence-level subject is admitted.
    evidence_ids:
    - owner-4
    - native-payloads
    - mem-specification
    - prior-scope
  details: This is not a claim of proven difference merely because the second catalogue
    number is absent. The specific P0440 evidence is narrower than the unresolved
    whole source/current generic owner. Preserve 0.89 g/8.9 g_l in solution 6374 and
    5.2 g/130 g_l in solution 4890 plus volume/preparation context. The 40 mL stored
    volume and step to 584 mL are not silently reconciled. Manufacturer 10.131 g/L
    reconstitution is not the microbial recipe dose. No bicarbonate/glutamine/form
    equivalence, external identifier, role, component decomposition or source split
    is approved.
- assessment_id: scope-and-namespace
  area: scope
  topic: Declared source completeness and namespace meaning
  outcome: supported
  target_ids:
  - source-1
  - source-2
  - source-3
  - source-4
  summary: The actual four-row canary includes every five matching occurrence from
    the complete copied source tables. Three MIMCMREFv1 IDs reconstruct from each
    exact original complete case plus the immutable declaration; they remain versioned
    MIM-generated material-reference scopes, not CultureMech-issued IDs or permanent
    ingredient IDs. The original 1879-case/1022-origin context remains bound and is
    not replaced by this canary.

- assessment_id: mapping-and-consumer-boundary
  area: graph
  topic: Permanent identity, inexact mappings and consumer namespace
  outcome: supported
  target_ids:
  - source-1
  - source-2
  - source-3
  - source-4
  summary: Current permanent IDs remain distinct from source reference IDs and legacy
    mapping identifiers. A supported barley identity needs no external ontology parent.
    Arabinan external broadMatch remains separately typed; beta-estradiol chemistry
    does not clear source holds; MEM remains without an invented external match. MIM.ingredient
    spelling preserves the same declared permanent URI as MIM and original CURIE provenance.
    Neither exact source identity nor any broader/close/narrow mapping authorizes
    subclass_of or owner collapse.

  - owner-2
  - owner-3
  - owner-4

findings:
- finding_id: ESTRADIOL-SOURCE-QUANTITY-HOLD
  issue_key: MIM-ESTRADIOL-1181-SOURCE-QUANTITY-HOLD
  category: quantity
  severity: major
  status: open
  certainty: confirmed
  title: Retained estradiol source dose and associated export obligation remain unresolved
  description: This is a current observation of the existing substantive hold, not
    a new owner-identity defect or a claim that the canary introduced it. NBRC 1181
    gives 1 mg with 1 L water; the immutable source occurrence says 1 g/L and includes
    an unapproved Growth factor note. Keep the proposed source assertion WITHHOLD,
    preserve both exact payloads, and obtain a separately authorized maintained-source
    correction and scientific review before lifting the obligation.
  target_ids:
  - source-3
  field_paths:
  - occurrences[0].row.original_occurrence_json.raw_component.concentration
  - holds
  evidence_ids:
  - estradiol-nbrc
  - estradiol-recipe
  - native-payloads
  - prior-scope
  rule_id: MAPPING_SEMANTICS reviewed source identities; original retained scientific
    hold
  normalization_reason: Material quantitative conflict plus explicit retained export
    obligation requires follow-up; beta-isomer identity evidence is not affected.
  owner_paths:
  - repository: CultureBotAI/CultureMech
    path: data/normalized_yaml/bacterial/1_10_brain_heart_infusion_agar_17_estradiol.yaml
    role: maintained upstream source quantity and role annotations
- finding_id: MEM-1923-WHOLE-SOURCE-IDENTITY-HOLD
  issue_key: MIM-MEM-1923-WHOLE-SOURCE-IDENTITY-HOLD
  category: identity
  severity: major
  status: open
  certainty: confirmed
  title: Retained whole-source MEM formulation identity requires scope reconciliation
  description: This retains the already-recorded unresolved whole-source obligation.
    Product evidence supports P0440 powder with Earle salts/L-glutamine and no bicarbonate,
    but current generic owner MIM:0002840 and both source occurrences have not been
    established as one exact formulation scope. The second BioWest-only occurrence
    does not by absence alone prove a different product. Do not split the source,
    select only the P0440 occurrence, infer a formulation, or clear the hold from
    a minted ID or historical imported source ID.
  target_ids:
  - source-4
  field_paths:
  - owner_record
  - occurrences
  - holds
  evidence_ids:
  - owner-4
  - source-occurrences
  - mem-specification
  - mem-product
  - mem-mediadive
  - prior-scope
  rule_id: MAPPING_SEMANTICS reviewed source identities; all-source-occurrence contract
  normalization_reason: Unresolved source/material scope blocks exact whole-source
    identity; it is not proof of a false current external mapping.
  owner_paths:
  - repository: CultureBotAI/MediaIngredientMech
    path: data/ingredients/unmapped/MEM_Alpha_Modification.yaml
    role: maintained generic MEM owner scope
actions:
- action_id: retain-and-resolve-estradiol-hold
  finding_ids:
  - ESTRADIOL-SOURCE-QUANTITY-HOLD
  target_ids:
  - source-3
  description: Retain the exact existing WITHHOLD obligation. If source correction
    is separately authorized, reconcile the normalized quantity and unsupported role
    annotation against NBRC while preserving the old frozen case; make a newly versioned
    complete source reference and independently review its new payload.
  ownership_note: CultureMech owns its normalized source record; this MIM source review
    changes neither repository.
  acceptance_checks:
  - Native review continues to withhold the old unchanged source payload while its
    hold remains.
  - Any new source payload preserves full historical provenance and receives independent
    whole-source scientific review; no owner identity or source hash is silently reused.
- action_id: retain-and-resolve-mem-hold
  finding_ids:
  - MEM-1923-WHOLE-SOURCE-IDENTITY-HOLD
  target_ids:
  - source-4
  description: Keep both captured MEM occurrences and the generic owner in withheld
    accounting until primary evidence resolves complete supplied formulation scope.
    Reconcile historical generic owner versus product-specific use without guessing,
    owner merging, or per-occurrence source invention.
  owner_paths:
  - repository: CultureBotAI/MediaIngredientMech
    path: data/ingredients/unmapped/MEM_Alpha_Modification.yaml
    role: maintained owner requiring formulation-specific reconciliation
  acceptance_checks:
  - Primary evidence supports or refutes one material scope across every occurrence
    and the exact current owner.
  - All original quantities/preparation context and historical holds remain visible;
    any proposed correction uses guarded native curation and new independent review.
limitations:
- This primary report does not substitute for the distinct skeptical reviewer, actual
  native source export validation, complete ordinary external SSSOM partition, publication
  technical acceptance or production/consumer authorization.
- All source-3/source-4 literal holds remain in native payloads. The new findings
  are observations of those retained obligations, not evidence that the current canary
  introduced them or authorization to close an old issue.
- Positive source-1/source-2 decisions do not authorize count-to-mass conversion,
  source-dose/role assertions, procurement-grade equivalence, taxonomic/food matching,
  generic-parent identity, owner merging or any other recipe annotations.
- The Arabinan manufacturer evidence is an archived extracted primary-content capture,
  not a new raw PDF download. It supports botanical material identity only; shared
  CAS never proves linear P-LARB and P-ARAB equivalence.
- Captured primary/authority text was inspected without fresh paid provider research.
  No live OAK term check, whole-corpus QC or future output validator is claimed here.
  Those global checks remain coordinator-owned and passed checks were not repeated.
- Complete accounting is established for the exact supplied tables. This does not
  certify completeness of any upstream provider database or turn a MIM-generated material
  reference into an upstream identifier.
- Current owner/source bytes withdraw old support; no prior W01/W09 report, old filename
  MIM alias or stable allocation is treated as current source identity approval.
notes:
- Native inspection ran after actual source inspection succeeded and after adoption;
  no provisional-stage vote was promoted by replacing hashes.
- Private readback retained setup error cadab3 (incorrect guessed stable_ids.py directory)
  and exploratory parse error 33ea92 (NBRC HTML stored with .json suffix); neither
  was a target-read denial or failed native scientific check. Correct file discovery
  and direct HTML reading succeeded before these judgments.
- No shared record-review check is run by this primary author; the coordinator runs
  it once after the actual primary and skeptical pairs exist.
- First unpublished serialization validation failed in tool07c747 because PyYAML emitted
  anchors for shared Python list objects. The original draft is retained. This successor
  expands those aliases without changing scientific conclusions, targets or inputs;
  native schema/helper were unchanged.
- Second unpublished validation576dc4 caught a review_id/updated-finish timestamp
  mismatch in the serialization successor. The ID is now derived from the actual stated
  finish timestamp, without changing conclusions, inputs or native checks; both earlier
  drafts remain.
tags:
- real-canary
- source-identity
- primary
- stable-ids
- complete-occurrences
- holds-retained
